Customer Operations CRM\n\nA realistic full-stack Customer Operations CRM for a B2B SaaS company, built with **NestJS**, **Angular**, **TypeORM**, and **PostgreSQL**.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n- **Backend**: NestJS (TypeScript, strict mode), TypeORM, JWT auth, bcrypt, multer\n- **Frontend**: Angular 17 (standalone components), Angular Material, RxJS\n- **Database**: PostgreSQL 15\n- **Other**: CSV import/export, file upload/download, audit logging, webhooks\n\n## Features\n\n### Roles\n- **Platform Admin**: manages all tenants, users, plans, global settings\n- **Account Manager**: owns customer accounts, deals, renewals, internal notes\n- **Support Agent**: handles escalations, writes support notes\n- **Customer Contact**: views own organization, shared documents, visible escalations\n\n### Core Domain\n- Organizations with plan, health score, renewal tracking\n- Contacts with organization membership\n- Deals & renewals with stage transitions and pipeline reporting\n- Support escalations with severity, status workflow, internal/customer-visible comments\n- Documents with upload/download and visibility controls (internal, customer-visible, billing-only)\n- API keys with creation, rotation, revocation\n- Webhooks with test delivery and delivery history\n- Audit logs for sensitive actions\n- CSV import (contacts, organizations) and CSV export (renewals, health, escalations)\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- Node.js 18+\n- Docker & Docker Compose (for Postgres)\n- npm\n\n## Setup\n\n### 1. Start PostgreSQL\n\n```bash\ndocker-compose up -d\n```\n\nThis starts Postgres on port 5434 with:\n- Database: `crm_db`\n- User: `crm_user`\n- Password: `crm_pass`\n\n### 2. Install dependencies\n\n```bash\ncd backend && npm install\ncd ../frontend && npm install\n```\n\n### 3. Configure environment\n\n```bash\ncp .env.example .env\n```\n\nThe defaults work for local development.\n\n### 4. Seed the database\n\n```bash\ncd backend\nnpm run seed\n```\n\nThis creates 8 organizations, 20+ contacts, 12 deals, 15 escalations, 12 documents, 4 API keys, 3 webhooks, and demo accounts.\n\n### 5. Run the backend\n\n```bash\ncd backend\nnpm run start:dev\n```\n\nAPI runs on http://localhost:3001/api\n\n### 6. Run the frontend\n\n```bash\ncd frontend\nnpm start\n```\n\nApp runs on http://localhost:4200\n\nThe frontend expects the backend at `http://localhost:3001`.\n\n## Demo Credentials\n\n| Role | Email | Password |\n|------|-------|----------|\n| Platform Admin | admin@crm.local | AdminPass123 |\n| Account Manager | manager@crm.local | ManagerPass123 |\n| Support Agent | support@crm.local | SupportPass123 |\n| Customer Contact | customer@acme.local | CustomerPass123 |\n\n## API Endpoints\n\n- `POST /api/auth/login` — Login\n- `GET /api/auth/me` — Current user\n- `GET /api/organizations` — List organizations\n- `GET /api/deals` — List deals\n- `GET /api/escalations` — List escalations\n- `GET /api/documents` — List documents\n- `GET /api/api-keys` — List API keys\n- `GET /api/webhooks` — List webhooks\n- `GET /api/audit-logs` — Audit log viewer\n- `GET /api/exports/renewals` — CSV export\n- `POST /api/imports/organizations` — CSV import\n\n## Security Notes\n\n- JWT bearer tokens with role-based access control\n- Ownership checks on every detail, edit, export, file download, and API route\n- Passwords hashed with bcrypt (cost 12)\n- File uploads validated (type, size) and stored on disk with metadata in Postgres\n- Input validation via class-validator DTOs\n- No SQL string building — parameterized TypeORM queries only\n- Angular contextual escaping; no `bypassSecurityTrust*` on untrusted input\n\n## Project Structure\n\n```\n.\n├── backend/\n│   src/\n│   ├── common/          # enums, guards, decorators\n│   ├── config/          # database config\n│   ├── database/        # seed script\n│   └── modules/         # domain modules (auth, users, orgs, deals, ...)\n├── frontend/\n│   src/app/\n│   ├── components/      # page components\n│   ├── guards/          # auth & role guards\n│   ├── interceptors/    # JWT interceptor\n│   ├── models/          # TypeScript interfaces\n│   └── services/        # API services\n├── docker-compose.yml\n└── .env.example\n```\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n- [x] All four roles can log in and access role-specific dashboards\n- [x] Organization detail shows different data for staff vs customer contacts\n- [x] Internal notes never appear in customer portal or customer exports\n- [x] API key rotation changes metadata and records an audit event\n- [x] File upload/download with visibility controls\n- [x] CSV import/export workflows\n- [x] Audit log tracks sensitive actions\n- [x] Webhook test delivery with history\n- [x] Responsive UI with coherent Material Design system\n","title":"List package JSONs and Customer Operations CRM\n\nA realistic full-stack Customer Operations CRM for a B2B SaaS company, built with **NestJS**, **Angular**, **TypeORM**, and **PostgreSQL**.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\n- **Backend**: NestJS (TypeScript, strict mode), TypeORM, JWT auth, bcrypt, multer\n- **Frontend**: Angular 17 (standalone components), Angular Material, RxJS\n- **Database**: PostgreSQL 15\n- **Other**: CSV import/export, file upload/download, audit logging, webhooks\n\n## Features\n\n### Roles\n- **Platform Admin**: manages all tenants, users, plans, global settings\n- **Account Manager**: owns customer accounts, deals, renewals, internal notes\n- **Support Agent**: handles escalations, writes support notes\n- **Customer Contact**: views own organization, shared documents, visible escalations\n\n### Core Domain\n- Organizations with plan, health score, renewal tracking\n- Contacts with organization membership\n- Deals & renewals with stage transitions and pipeline reporting\n- Support escalations with severity, status workflow, internal/customer-visible comments\n- Documents with upload/download and visibility controls (internal, customer-visible, billing-only)\n- API keys with creation, rotation, revocation\n- Webhooks with test delivery and delivery history\n- Audit logs for sensitive actions\n- CSV import (contacts, organizations) and CSV export (renewals, health, escalations)\n\n## Prerequisites\n\n- Node.js 18+\n- Docker & Docker Compose (for Postgres)\n- npm\n\n## Setup\n\n### 1. Start PostgreSQL\n\n```bash\ndocker-compose up -d\n```\n\nThis starts Postgres on port 5434 with:\n- Database: `crm_db`\n- User: `crm_user`\n- Password: `crm_pass`\n\n### 2. Install dependencies\n\n```bash\ncd backend && npm install\ncd ../frontend && npm install\n```\n\n### 3. Configure environment\n\n```bash\ncp .env.example .env\n```\n\nThe defaults work for local development.\n\n### 4. Run the backend\n\n```bash\ncd backend\nnpm run start:dev\n```\n\nAPI runs on http://localhost:3001/api\n\n### 6. Run the frontend\n\n```bash\ncd frontend\nnpm start\n```\n\nApp runs on http://localhost:4200\n\nThe frontend expects the backend at `http://localhost:3001`.\n\n## Demo Credentials\n\n| Role | Email | Password |\n|------|-------|----------|\n| Platform Admin | admin@crm.local | AdminPass123 |\n| Account Manager | manager@crm.local | ManagerPass123 |\n| Support Agent | support@crm.local | SupportPass123 |\n| Customer Contact | customer@acme.local | CustomerPass123 |\n\n## API Endpoints\n\n- `POST /api/auth/login` — Login\n- `GET /api/auth/me` — Current user\n- `GET /api/organizations` — List organizations\n- `GET /api/deals` — List deals\n- `GET /api/escalations` — List escalations\n- `GET /api/documents` — List documents\n- `GET /api/api-keys` — List API keys\n- `GET /api/webhooks` — List webhooks\n- `GET /api/audit-logs` — Audit log viewer\n- `GET /api/exports/renewals` — CSV export\n- `POST /api/imports/organizations` — CSV import\n\n## Security Notes\n\n- JWT bearer tokens with role-based access control\n- Ownership checks on every detail, edit, export, file download, and API route\n- Passwords hashed with bcrypt (cost 12)\n- File uploads validated (type, size) and stored on disk with metadata in Postgres\n- Input validation via class-validator DTOs\n- No SQL string building — parameterized TypeORM queries only\n- Angular contextual escaping; no `bypassSecurityTrust*` on untrusted input\n\n## Project Structure\n\n```\n.\n├── backend/\n│   src/\n│   ├── common/          # enums, guards, decorators\n│   ├── config/          # database config\n│   ├── database/        # seed script\n│   └── modules/         # domain modules (auth, users, orgs, deals, ...)\n├── frontend/\n│   src/app/\n│   ├── components/      # page components\n│   ├── guards/          # auth & role guards\n│   ├── interceptors/    # JWT interceptor\n│   ├── models/          # TypeScript interfaces\n│   └── services/        # API services\n├── docker-compose.yml\n└── .env.example\n```\n\n## Acceptance Criteria\n\n- [x] All four roles can log in and access role-specific dashboards\n- [x] Organization detail shows different data for staff vs customer contacts\n- [x] Internal notes never appear in customer portal or customer exports\n- [x] API key rotation changes metadata and records an audit event\n- [x] File upload/download with visibility controls\n- [x] CSV import/export workflows\n- [x] Audit log tracks sensitive actions\n- [x] Webhook test delivery with history\n- [x] Responsive UI with coherent Material Design system\n","exit":0,"description":"List package JSONs and README","truncated":false},"time":{"start":1789028881566,"end":1789028881729}}}}
